data-team-hovercards-enabled="true" class="markdown-body" data-turbolinks="false"><div class="markdown-body safe-html-box__Uwc6Al Container__ZAxOBo"><p dir="auto">Hello dear maintainers,</p>
<p dir="auto">we are using Quarkus CXF 3.26.1 with WS-Security.</p>
<p dir="auto">However when building natively, we noticed that <em>quarkus-cxf-rt-ws-security</em> pulls in <em>cxf-core</em> which in return causes through reflection the build to fail as <strong>SpringBusFactory</strong> is attempted to be initialised.</p>
<p dir="auto">My limited understanding of <em>quarkus-cxf</em> tells me that there are suitable replacements in form of <em>QuarkusBusFactory</em>.</p>
<p dir="auto">We tried to exclude the assumed unwanted Maven dependency onto <em>cxf-core</em> without luck.</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="   &lt;dependency&gt;
     &lt;groupId&gt;io.quarkiverse.cxf&lt;/groupId&gt;
     &lt;artifactId&gt;quarkus-cxf-rt-ws-security&lt;/artifactId&gt;
     &lt;exclusions&gt;
       &lt;exclusion&gt;
         &lt;groupId&gt;org.apache.cxf&lt;/groupId&gt;
         &lt;artifactId&gt;cxf-core&lt;/artifactId&gt;
        &lt;/exclusion&gt;
     &lt;/exclusions&gt;
   &lt;/dependency&gt;"><pre class="notranslate"><code class="notranslate">   &lt;dependency&gt;
     &lt;groupId&gt;io.quarkiverse.cxf&lt;/groupId&gt;
     &lt;artifactId&gt;quarkus-cxf-rt-ws-security&lt;/artifactId&gt;
     &lt;exclusions&gt;
       &lt;exclusion&gt;
         &lt;groupId&gt;org.apache.cxf&lt;/groupId&gt;
         &lt;artifactId&gt;cxf-core&lt;/artifactId&gt;
        &lt;/exclusion&gt;
     &lt;/exclusions&gt;
   &lt;/dependency&gt;
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Further investigation shows that there are imports on <code class="notranslate">org.apache.cxf.Bus</code> et. al. in <em>HTTPConduitImpl</em>;</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="    import org.apache.cxf.Bus;
    import org.apache.cxf.configuration.jsse.TLSClientParameters;
    import org.apache.cxf.service.model.EndpointInfo;
    import org.apache.cxf.transport.http.HTTPConduit;
    import org.apache.cxf.transport.http.URLConnectionHTTPConduit;
    import org.apache.cxf.ws.addressing.EndpointReferenceType;

    import io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client.HttpClientPool;
    import io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client.VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit;
    import io.quarkus.runtime.annotations.ConfigDocEnumValue;

    public enum HTTPConduitImpl implements HTTPConduitSpec {"><pre class="notranslate"><code class="notranslate">    import org.apache.cxf.Bus;
    import org.apache.cxf.configuration.jsse.TLSClientParameters;
    import org.apache.cxf.service.model.EndpointInfo;
    import org.apache.cxf.transport.http.HTTPConduit;
    import org.apache.cxf.transport.http.URLConnectionHTTPConduit;
    import org.apache.cxf.ws.addressing.EndpointReferenceType;

    import io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client.HttpClientPool;
    import io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client.VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit;
    import io.quarkus.runtime.annotations.ConfigDocEnumValue;

    public enum HTTPConduitImpl implements HTTPConduitSpec {
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">which should be actually pulled from <em>quarkus-cxf</em>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Let me know if you need further details to this issue. Apart from the above Maven depedency we only use a <em>UsernameTokenPasswordServerCallback</em> as per documentation.</p>
<p dir="auto">Best regards,<br>
Ferdinand</p></div></div><div class="IssueBodyTaskList__-FlQre"><div role="toolbar" aria-label="Reactions" class="d-flex gap-1 flex-wrap"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><!--$--><div actions</span></div></div></div></div></div><div class="Body__scgEjp IssueCommentBody__DGWd7U"><div data-testid="markdown-body" data-team-hovercards-enabled="true" class="markdown-body" data-turbolinks="false"><div class="markdown-body safe-html-box__Uwc6Al Container__ZAxOBo"><p dir="auto">We run the same tests with and <a href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/blob/main/.github/actions/run-native-test/action.yml#L33-L49">without</a> <code class="notranslate">QUARKUS_CXF_DEFAULT_HTTP_CONDUIT_FACTORY=URLConnectionHTTPConduitFactory</code>.<br>
It is because we support two HttpConduit factories: <code class="notranslate">VertxHttpClientHTTPConduitFactory</code> (default) and <code class="notranslate">URLConnectionHTTPConduitFactory</code>. Those basically wrap two different HTTP client implementations for CXF.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you do not set any of the <a href="https://docs.quarkiverse.io/quarkus-cxf/dev/reference/extensions/quarkus-cxf.html#quarkus-cxf_quarkus-cxf-http-conduit-factory" rel="nofollow"><code class="notranslate">quarkus.cxf.*http-conduit-factory</code> properties</a>, then  you use <code class="notranslate">VertxHttpClientHTTPConduitFactory</code> and thus the test invocation without <code class="notranslate">QUARKUS_CXF_DEFAULT_HTTP_CONDUIT_FACTORY</code> should be relevatn for you.</p></div></div><div role="toolbar" aria-label="Reactions" class="d-flex gap-1 flex-wrap"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></li><li data-wrapper-timeline-id="IC_kwDOElz7Sc7Jy0oQ"><div class="timelineElement__l_1Q03 nonLeadingElement_inlineAvatars__g_btmU"><div I used the following trick:</p>
<p dir="auto">`import com.oracle.svm.core.annotate.Substitute;<br>
import com.oracle.svm.core.annotate.TargetClass;</p>
<p dir="auto">public class CxfNativeFix {</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@TargetClass(className = &quot;org.apache.cxf.ws.security.trust.STSLoginModule&quot;)
final class Target_STSLoginModule {
@Substitute
    public boolean login() {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException(&quot;STSLoginModule is not supported in Quarkus Native&quot;);
    }
}"><pre class="notranslate"><code class="notranslate">@TargetClass(className = "org.apache.cxf.ws.security.trust.STSLoginModule")
final class Target_STSLoginModule {
@Substitute
    public boolean login() {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("STSLoginModule is not supported in Quarkus Native");
    }
}
</code></pre></div>
